L ```console

T 13 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown

L ## Using the Cobra Generator
T 2 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.2.markdown punctuation.definition.heading.markdown
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.2.markdown
T 28 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.2.markdown entity.name.section.markdown
L 
L Cobra-CLI is its own program that will create your application and add any commands you want.
T 93 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L It's the easiest way to incorporate Cobra into your application.
T 64 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L 
L For complete details on using the Cobra generator, please refer to [The Cobra-CLI Generator README](https://github.com/spf13/cobra-cli/blob/main/README.md)

L ### Organizing subcommands
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.3.markdown punctuation.definition.heading.markdown
T 4 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.3.markdown
T 26 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.3.markdown entity.name.section.markdown
L 
L A command may have subcommands which in turn may have other subcommands. This is achieved by using
T 98 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L `AddCommand`. In some cases, especially in larger applications, each subcommand may be defined in
T 1 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 11 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown
T 12 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 97 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L its own go package.
T 19 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L 
L The suggested approach is for the parent command to use `AddCommand` to add its most immediate
T 56 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
T 57 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 67 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown
T 68 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 94 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L subcommands. For example, consider the following directory structure:
T 69 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L 
L ```console
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown punctuation.definition.markdown
T 10 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown fenced_code.block.language
L ├── cmd
T 13 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L │   ├── root.go
T 25 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L │   └── sub1
T 22 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L │       ├── sub1.go
T 29 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L │       └── sub2
T 26 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L │           ├── leafA.go
T 34 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L │           ├── leafB.go
T 34 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L │           └── sub2.go
T 33 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L └── main.go
T 17 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L ```
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown punctuation.definition.markdown

L ## Help Command
T 2 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.2.markdown punctuation.definition.heading.markdown
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.2.markdown
T 15 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.2.markdown entity.name.section.markdown
L 
L Cobra automatically adds a help command to your application when you have subcommands.
T 86 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L This will be called when a user runs 'app help'. Additionally, help will also
T 77 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L support all other commands as input. Say, for instance, you have a command called
T 81 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L 'create' without any additional configuration; Cobra will work when 'app help
T 77 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L create' is called.  Every command will automatically have the '--help' flag added.
T 82 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L 
L ### Example
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.3.markdown punctuation.definition.heading.markdown
T 4 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.3.markdown
T 11 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.3.markdown entity.name.section.markdown
L 
L The following output is automatically generated by Cobra. Nothing beyond the
T 76 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L command and flag definitions are needed.
T 40 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L 
L ```console
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown punctuation.definition.markdown
T 10 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown fenced_code.block.language
L $ cobra-cli help
T 16 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L 
L Cobra is a CLI library for Go that empowers applications.
T 57 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L This application is a tool to generate the needed files
T 55 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L to quickly create a Cobra application.
T 38 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L 
L Usage:
T 6 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L   cobra-cli [command]
T 21 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L 
L Available Commands:
T 19 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L   add         Add a command to a Cobra Application
T 50 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L   completion  Generate the autocompletion script for the specified shell
T 72 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L   help        Help about any command
T 36 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L   init        Initialize a Cobra Application
T 44 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L 
L Flags:
T 6 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L   -a, --author string    author name for copyright attribution (default "YOUR NAME")
T 84 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L       --config string    config file (default is $HOME/.cobra.yaml)
T 67 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L   -h, --help             help for cobra-cli
T 43 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L   -l, --license string   name of license for the project
T 56 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L       --viper            use Viper for configuration
T 52 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L 
L Use "cobra-cli [command] --help" for more information about a command.
T 70 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L ```
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown punctuation.definition.markdown
L 
L Help is just a command like any other. There is no special logic or behavior
T 76 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L around it. In fact, you can provide your own if you want.
T 57 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L 
L ### Grouping commands in help
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.3.markdown punctuation.definition.heading.markdown
T 4 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.3.markdown
T 29 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.3.markdown entity.name.section.markdown
L 
L Cobra supports grouping of available commands in the help output.  To group commands, each group must be explicitly
T 115 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L defined using `AddGroup()` on the parent command.  Then a subcommand can be added to a group using the `GroupID` element
T 14 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
T 15 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 25 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown
T 26 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 103 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
T 104 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 111 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown
T 112 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 120 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L of that subcommand. The groups will appear in the help output in the same order as they are defined using different
T 115 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L calls to `AddGroup()`.  If you use the generated `help` or `completion` commands, you can set their group ids using
T 9 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
T 10 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 20 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown
T 21 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 49 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
T 50 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 54 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown
T 55 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 59 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
T 60 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 70 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown
T 71 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 115 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L `SetHelpCommandGroupId()` and `SetCompletionCommandGroupId()` on the root command, respectively.
T 1 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 24 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown
T 25 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 30 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
T 31 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 60 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown
T 61 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
T 96 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown

L ## Usage Message
T 2 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.2.markdown punctuation.definition.heading.markdown
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.2.markdown
T 16 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.2.markdown entity.name.section.markdown
L 
L When the user provides an invalid flag or invalid command, Cobra responds by
T 76 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L showing the user the 'usage'.
T 29 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L 
L ### Example
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.3.markdown punctuation.definition.heading.markdown
T 4 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.3.markdown
T 11 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.3.markdown entity.name.section.markdown
L You may recognize this from the help above. That's because the default help
T 75 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L embeds the usage as part of its output.
T 39 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
L 
L ```console
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown punctuation.definition.markdown
T 10 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown fenced_code.block.language
L $ cobra-cli --invalid
T 21 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L Error: unknown flag: --invalid
T 30 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L Usage:
T 6 text.html.markdown markup.fenced_code.block.markdown
L   cobra-cli [command]

L ## PreRun and PostRun Hooks
T 2 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.2.markdown punctuation.definition.heading.markdown
T 3 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.2.markdown
T 27 text.html.markdown markup.heading.markdown heading.2.markdown entity.name.section.markdown
L 
L It is possible to run functions before or after the main `Run` function of your command. The `PersistentPreRun` and `PreRun` functions will be executed before `Run`. `PersistentPostRun` and `PostRun` will be executed after `Run`.  The `Persistent*Run` functions will be inherited by children if they do not declare their own.  The `*PreRun` and `*PostRun` functions will only be executed if the `Run` function of the current command has been declared.  These functions are run in the following order:
T 57 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown
T 58 text.html.markdown meta.paragraph.markdown markup.inline.raw.string.markdown punctuation.definition.raw.markdown
